David F. DeCoste

Republican   5th Plymouth  ·  House of Representatives
Recorded votes 2015 to 2026, 189th to 194th General Court. The record on this page begins where the readable record does, not necessarily where this member's service did.

Record card

Counts beside context, not grades. "Typical member" is the member in the middle of this chamber that session, when everyone is lined up by the same measure. The party column is the same, among members of the party this member belonged to in that session; where the legislature's roster names no party for the session, as it does not for 2009 and 2010, that column is empty. Nobody is ranked. How each measure is counted, and what it cannot tell you.

Counts here are a list, not a score: a chamber that records few votes, and records them mostly when it already agrees, makes any rate from this list a fact about the chamber's rules before it is a fact about the member.
